a2db903138eefa7ce53c542a903862f68f1634fda436bd29a2d06ab681d5dd20  /home/girish/THESIS-2026/CLASSIFICATION GIT/MLKEM_CBMC_FULL_CLASSIFIED_BASELINE_2026-07-19/experiments/poly-sub/T06-callsite-integration/raw-evidence/SUB00R_BATCH6_T6_CALLSITE_INTEGRATION/00_PREREGISTRATION/SUB_T6_B6_0_PREREGISTRATION.md

     1	# SUB-T6 / Batch 6 Preregistration
     2	
     3	## 1. Record identity
     4	
     5	- Campaign: `mlk_poly_sub clean-room verification`
     6	- Batch: `SUB-T6 / Batch 6`
     7	- Gate: `B6.0`
     8	- Schema: `sub-t6-b6.0-preregistration-v1`
     9	- Status: `FROZEN`
    10	
    11	This record was created before any Batch-6 harness construction,
    12	GOTO construction, CBMC execution, reachability result, expected-failure
    13	result or mutation result was observed.
    14	
    15	## 2. Official theorem
    16	
    17	```text
    18	SUB-T6:
    19	
    20	Production Call-Site Contract Satisfaction and Subtract–Reduce Handoff Correctness of mlk_poly_sub in mlk_indcpa_dec
    21	```
    22	
    23	## 3. Production boundary
    24	
    25	The upstream-to-downstream production boundary is:
    26	
    27	```c
    28	mlk_poly_invntt_tomont(sb);
    29	mlk_poly_sub(v, sb);
    30	mlk_poly_reduce(v);
    31	mlk_poly_tomsg(m, v);
    32	```
    33	
    34	The actual bounded slice intended for execution is:
    35	
    36	```c
    37	mlk_poly_sub(v, sb);
    38	mlk_poly_reduce(v);
    39	mlk_poly_tomsg(m, v);
    40	```
    41	
    42	Immediately preceding operations are represented through their frozen
    43	contract or postcondition interfaces. Batch 6 does not reprove those
    44	upstream cryptographic operations.
    45	
    46	## 4. Frozen parameters and arithmetic domain
    47	
    48	- `MLKEM_N = 256`
    49	- `MLKEM_Q = 3329`
    50	- `MLK_INVNTT_BOUND = 26632`
    51	- `v` interval: `[0, 3328]`
    52	- `sb` interval: `[-26631, 26631]`
    53	- Derived subtraction interval: `[-26631, 29959]`
    54	- Signed `int16_t` interval: `[-32768, 32767]`
    55	
    56	The subtraction interval must be derived from the upstream bounds; it
    57	must not be introduced as an independent harness assumption.
    58	
    59	## 5. Permitted assumptions
    60	
    61	1. 0 <= v_before[i] < 3329 for every coefficient.
    62	2. abs(sb_before[i]) < 26632 for every coefficient.
    63	3. successful allocation path.
    64	4. v and sb are valid complete distinct non-overlapping mlk_poly objects.
    65	5. each polynomial has exactly 256 coefficients.
    66	6. upstream coefficient bounds are compositional interfaces.
    67	7. exact source configuration contracts and tool model are frozen later.
    68	
    69	## 6. Prohibited assumptions and transformations
    70	
    71	1. `assume(false)`.
    72	2. `unreachable target calls`.
    73	3. `assume subtraction fits int16_t`.
    74	4. `assume derived range [-26631,29959]`.
    75	5. `assume exact mlk_poly_sub output`.
    76	6. `assume canonical mlk_poly_reduce output`.
    77	7. `assume mlk_poly_tomsg precondition`.
    78	8. `exclude coefficient indices 0 or 255`.
    79	9. `replace target functions with conclusion-establishing stubs`.
    80	10. `modify frozen production implementation for positive proof`.
    81	11. `silently disable safety checks or unwinding assertions`.
    82	
    83	Mathematical subtraction checks must use a widened integer type before
    84	comparison with the signed 16-bit limits.
    85	
    86	## 7. Derived arithmetic obligation
    87	
    88	```text
    89	minimum = 0 - 26631 = -26631
    90	maximum = 3328 - (-26631) = 29959
    91	
    92	-26631 <= v[i] - sb[i] <= 29959
    93	[-26631, 29959] is contained in [-32768, 32767]
    94	```
    95	
    96	This representability statement is a proof obligation, not an assumption.
    97	
    98	## 8. Registered positive properties
    99	
   100	### T6.1 - Production call-site object validity and separation
   101	
   102	Prove valid, complete, distinct and non-aliasing call operands
   103	under the successful allocation-path model.
   104	
   105	### T6.2 - Derived subtraction representability
   106	
   107	Derive signed 16-bit subtraction representability from only the
   108	registered upstream coefficient bounds.
   109	
   110	### T6.3 - Exact call-site subtraction result
   111	
   112	Execute the actual production `mlk_poly_sub` and prove exact
   113	coefficient-wise subtraction over the caller-derived domain.
   114	
   115	### T6.4 - Source and named caller-frame preservation
   116	
   117	Prove preservation of `sb` and each explicitly registered
   118	harness-observed caller-owned guard object.
   119	
   120	### T6.5 - Subtraction-to-reduction handoff correctness
   121	
   122	Execute the actual subtraction and reduction functions and prove
   123	that the reduction output is canonical.
   124	
   125	### T6.6 - Downstream mlk_poly_tomsg precondition satisfaction
   126	
   127	Prove the canonical-input requirement of `mlk_poly_tomsg` before
   128	executing the actual downstream consumer.
   129	
   130	### T6.7 - Safety of the complete bounded integration slice
   131	
   132	Prove safety and complete unwinding of the bounded production
   133	slice under the registered CBMC checks.
   134	
   135	## 9. Planned positive harness family
   136	
   137	- `sub_t6_callsite_precondition_harness.c`
   138	- `sub_t6_callsite_exactness_harness.c`
   139	- `sub_t6_callsite_frame_harness.c`
   140	- `sub_t6_sub_reduce_handoff_harness.c`
   141	- `sub_t6_tomsg_precondition_harness.c`
   142	
   143	## 10. Required reachability controls
   144	
   145	### R6.1 - Lower subtraction boundary
   146	
   147	```text
   148	v = 0
   149	sb = 26631
   150	result = -26631
   151	```
   152	
   153	### R6.2 - Upper subtraction boundary
   154	
   155	```text
   156	v = 3328
   157	sb = -26631
   158	result = 29959
   159	```
   160	
   161	### R6.3 - Neutral subtraction
   162	
   163	```text
   164	v = 0
   165	sb = 0
   166	result = 0
   167	```
   168	
   169	### R6.4 - Source-sign reachability
   170	
   171	- `positive` source coefficient
   172	- `negative` source coefficient
   173	
   174	### R6.5 - Reduction-input classes
   175	
   176	- `negative`
   177	- `zero`
   178	- `canonical`
   179	- `at_least_q`
   180	
   181	### R6.6 - Index boundaries
   182	
   183	- index `0`
   184	- index `127`
   185	- index `255`
   186	
   187	## 11. Expected-failure controls
   188	
   189	### EF-T6-1 - False overflow-existence claim
   190	
   191	The intended false property must fail. Compilation, unwinding or
   192	unrelated safety failures do not satisfy this control.
   193	
   194	### EF-T6-2 - False canonicalisation-failure claim
   195	
   196	The intended false property must fail. Compilation, unwinding or
   197	unrelated safety failures do not satisfy this control.
   198	
   199	### EF-T6-3 - False downstream-incompatibility claim
   200	
   201	The intended false property must fail. Compilation, unwinding or
   202	unrelated safety failures do not satisfy this control.
   203	
   204	## 12. Mandatory mutation controls
   205	
   206	### M6.1 - replace subtraction with addition
   207	
   208	- Expected detector: `T6.3`
   209	- The mutant must compile and produce a valid GOTO model.
   210	- The mutated behaviour must be reachable.
   211	- The intended property must fail without unrelated failures.
   212	- An interpretable counterexample must be retained.
   213	
   214	### M6.2 - remove mlk_poly_reduce
   215	
   216	- Expected detector: `T6.6`
   217	- The mutant must compile and produce a valid GOTO model.
   218	- The mutated behaviour must be reachable.
   219	- The intended property must fail without unrelated failures.
   220	- An interpretable counterexample must be retained.
   221	
   222	### M6.3 - modify source operand sb
   223	
   224	- Expected detector: `T6.4`
   225	- The mutant must compile and produce a valid GOTO model.
   226	- The mutated behaviour must be reachable.
   227	- The intended property must fail without unrelated failures.
   228	- An interpretable counterexample must be retained.
   229	
   230	### M6.4 - skip coefficient 255
   231	
   232	- Expected detector: `T6.3, T6.5`
   233	- The mutant must compile and produce a valid GOTO model.
   234	- The mutated behaviour must be reachable.
   235	- The intended property must fail without unrelated failures.
   236	- An interpretable counterexample must be retained.
   237	
   238	## 13. Stretch mutation control
   239	
   240	### M6.5 - call mlk_poly_tomsg before reduction
   241	
   242	- Expected detector: `T6.6`
   243	
   244	## 14. Required CBMC checks
   245	
   246	- `array-bounds`
   247	- `pointer`
   248	- `pointer-primitive`
   249	- `pointer-overflow`
   250	- `signed-overflow`
   251	- `unsigned-overflow`
   252	- `conversion`
   253	- `undefined-shift`
   254	- `division-by-zero`
   255	- `unwinding-assertions`
   256	
   257	Any unavailable or inapplicable option must be explicitly documented and
   258	justified rather than silently omitted.
   259	
   260	## 15. Permitted final claim
   261	
   262	> Under the frozen ML-KEM configuration, successful allocation-separation
   263	> model and registered upstream coefficient bounds, CBMC verified that the
   264	> actual bounded `mlk_poly_sub` to `mlk_poly_reduce` to `mlk_poly_tomsg`
   265	> production slice satisfies the registered representability, exactness,
   266	> frame, reduction-handoff, downstream-precondition and safety properties,
   267	> subject to the recorded assumptions, loop bounds, tool model and
   268	> source/build bindings.
   269	
   270	## 16. Frozen non-claims
   271	
   272	- complete K-PKE.Decrypt correctness.
   273	- correct plaintext for every ciphertext.
   274	- NTT or inverse-NTT correctness.
   275	- base multiplication correctness.
   276	- ciphertext decompression correctness.
   277	- secret-key unpacking correctness.
   278	- every allocator implementation.
   279	- out-of-memory paths unless separately modelled.
   280	- constant-time execution or side-channel freedom.
   281	- whole-library whole-program or end-to-end ML-KEM correctness.
   282	- unbounded correctness or unregistered configurations.
   283	
   284	## 17. Frozen gate order
   285	
   286	1. `B6.0 preregistration`
   287	2. `B6.1 call-site and contract binding`
   288	3. `B6.2 arithmetic and assumption audit`
   289	4. `B6.3 harness construction and freeze`
   290	5. `B6.4 structural and GOTO preflight`
   291	6. `B6.5 positive execution`
   292	7. `B6.6 reachability`
   293	8. `B6.7 expected failures`
   294	9. `B6.8 mutations`
   295	10. `B6.9 final evidence`
   296	
   297	Later gates may strengthen evidence but may not silently weaken the
   298	registered theorem, remove a property or introduce a prohibited assumption.
   299	
   300	## 18. Execution state at preregistration
   301	
   302	- `batch5_modified`: `FALSE`
   303	- `cbmc_executed`: `FALSE`
   304	- `goto_constructed`: `FALSE`
   305	- `harness_constructed`: `FALSE`
   306	- `production_modified`: `FALSE`
   307	- `results_observed`: `FALSE`
   308	
   309	## 19. Evidence-handling rules
   310	
   311	- Terminal-first operation remains active.
   312	- Files are requested only at important freeze or inspection gates.
   313	- Production source remains unmodified.
   314	- Batch 5 remains untouched.
   315	- Raw evidence is retained alongside summaries.
   316	- Hashes bind frozen evidence.
   317	- No inconvenient or failing evidence may be silently removed.

     1	# SUB-T6 B6.3 — Frozen Call-Site Integration Harness Family
     2	
     3	## Status
     4	
     5	FROZEN after static validation.
     6	
     7	## Positive harnesses
     8	
     9	1. sub_t6_callsite_precondition_harness.c — T6.1 and T6.2.
    10	2. sub_t6_callsite_exactness_harness.c — T6.3.
    11	3. sub_t6_callsite_frame_harness.c — T6.4.
    12	4. sub_t6_sub_reduce_handoff_harness.c — T6.5.
    13	5. sub_t6_tomsg_precondition_harness.c — T6.6 and the T6.7 slice boundary.
    14	
    15	## Exact production functions
    16	
    17	- mlk_poly_sub from frozen source/mlkem/src/poly.c.
    18	- mlk_poly_reduce from frozen source/mlkem/src/poly.c.
    19	- mlk_poly_tomsg from frozen source/mlkem/src/compress.c.
    20	
    21	## Domain model
    22	
    23	- 0 <= v[i] < 3329.
    24	- -26632 < sb[i] < 26632.
    25	- Signed-16 representability is asserted after widened subtraction; it is not assumed.
    26	- Complete distinct automatic objects instantiate the successful allocation path.
    27	- Allocator correctness and out-of-memory behavior are not proved.
    28	
    29	## Support provenance
    30	
    31	The zero opt-blocker support translation unit is copied byte-for-byte from the
    32	validated frozen Batch-5 family. Its source and copied hashes are recorded.
    33	The Batch-6 common, pragma and fail-closed zeroize support files are new
    34	Batch-6 artefacts.
    35	
    36	## Boundary
    37	
    38	This family contains no modified production source and is not CBMC proof
    39	evidence. GOTO construction and model inspection occur in B6.4.

     1	# SUB-T6 final verification verdict
     2	
     3	The SUB-T6 campaign passed for the frozen ML-KEM-768 configuration.
     4	
     5	The positive proof suite established T6.1–T6.7 for the registered bounded
     6	production slice. The campaign recorded 2343 successful positive
     7	CBMC properties, 365 successful reachability-companion
     8	properties, 12/12 non-vacuity covers, three isolated
     9	expected-failure controls, and a mandatory mutation score of 4/4.
    10	
    11	Removing `mlk_poly_reduce` was detected by both registered T6.6 canonical-input
    12	bounds. The same mutant also produced the documented downstream conversion
    13	failure `mlk_scalar_compress_d1.overflow.1`, because the deliberately
    14	noncanonical negative value was passed to `mlk_poly_tomsg`. This collateral
    15	failure is preserved and classified separately; it is not counted as an
    16	unrelated failure.
    17	
    18	This remains a property-specific bounded proof, not a proof of complete K-PKE
    19	decryption, all upstream cryptographic operations, allocator failure paths,
    20	constant-time behaviour, side-channel freedom, or end-to-end ML-KEM.

     1	/* SUB-T6 positive harness: T6.4. */
     2	#include "sub00r_b6_harness_common.h"
     3	
     4	int main(void)
     5	{
     6	  mlk_poly v;
     7	  mlk_poly sb;
     8	  mlk_poly v_before;
     9	  mlk_poly sb_before;
    10	  mlk_poly sb_witness;
    11	  mlk_poly guard;
    12	  mlk_poly guard_before;
    13	  unsigned i;
    14	
    15	  sub_t6_check_machine_model();
    16	  sub_t6_assume_callsite_inputs(&v, &sb);
    17	
    18	  for (i = 0u; i < MLKEM_N; i++)
    19	  {
    20	    guard.coeffs[i] = (int16_t)(255 - (int)i);
    21	  }
    22	
    23	  v_before = v;
    24	  sb_before = sb;
    25	  sb_witness = sb_before;
    26	  guard_before = guard;
    27	
    28	  mlk_poly_sub(&v, &sb);
    29	
    30	  for (i = 0u; i < MLKEM_N; i++)
    31	  {
    32	    int32_t expected;
    33	
    34	    expected = (int32_t)v_before.coeffs[i] -
    35	               (int32_t)sb_before.coeffs[i];
    36	
    37	    __CPROVER_assert((int32_t)v.coeffs[i] == expected,
    38	                     "SUB_T6_T6_4_ANCHOR: production subtraction must be exact");
    39	    __CPROVER_assert(sb.coeffs[i] == sb_before.coeffs[i],
    40	                     "SUB_T6_T6_4_SB: source sb must remain unchanged");
    41	    __CPROVER_assert(sb_before.coeffs[i] == sb_witness.coeffs[i],
    42	                     "SUB_T6_T6_4_SNAPSHOT: saved sb snapshot must remain unchanged");
    43	    __CPROVER_assert(guard.coeffs[i] == guard_before.coeffs[i],
    44	                     "SUB_T6_T6_4_GUARD: unrelated caller-owned guard must remain unchanged");
    45	  }
    46	
    47	  return 0;
    48	}

     1	/* SUB-T6 positive harness: T6.1 and T6.2. */
     2	#include "sub00r_b6_harness_common.h"
     3	
     4	int main(void)
     5	{
     6	  mlk_poly v;
     7	  mlk_poly sb;
     8	  mlk_poly v_before;
     9	  mlk_poly sb_before;
    10	  unsigned i;
    11	
    12	  sub_t6_check_machine_model();
    13	  sub_t6_assume_callsite_inputs(&v, &sb);
    14	
    15	  __CPROVER_assert((void *)&v != (void *)&sb,
    16	                   "SUB_T6_T6_1: v and sb must be distinct objects");
    17	  __CPROVER_assert(sizeof(v) == sizeof(mlk_poly),
    18	                   "SUB_T6_T6_1: v must be a complete polynomial object");
    19	  __CPROVER_assert(sizeof(sb) == sizeof(mlk_poly),
    20	                   "SUB_T6_T6_1: sb must be a complete polynomial object");
    21	
    22	  v_before = v;
    23	  sb_before = sb;
    24	
    25	  for (i = 0u; i < MLKEM_N; i++)
    26	  {
    27	    int32_t d;
    28	
    29	    d = (int32_t)v_before.coeffs[i] -
    30	        (int32_t)sb_before.coeffs[i];
    31	
    32	    __CPROVER_assert(d >= INT16_MIN,
    33	                     "SUB_T6_T6_2_LOWER: subtraction must fit int16_t");
    34	    __CPROVER_assert(d <= INT16_MAX,
    35	                     "SUB_T6_T6_2_UPPER: subtraction must fit int16_t");
    36	  }
    37	
    38	  mlk_poly_sub(&v, &sb);
    39	
    40	  for (i = 0u; i < MLKEM_N; i++)
    41	  {
    42	    int32_t d;
    43	
    44	    d = (int32_t)v_before.coeffs[i] -
    45	        (int32_t)sb_before.coeffs[i];
    46	
    47	    __CPROVER_assert((int32_t)v.coeffs[i] == d,
    48	                     "SUB_T6_T6_2_ANCHOR: actual call must realize derived subtraction");
    49	  }
    50	
    51	  return 0;
    52	}

     8	  mlk_poly v_before;

1384:## 24.5 Recommended novelty claim
1386:The recommended thesis claim is:
1388:> The contribution is not a first proof of ML-KEM polynomial addition or subtraction. It is a controlled case study of AI-assisted generation and human/tool validation of caller-oriented CBMC artefacts for a frozen high-assurance implementation. The distinctive element is the integrated evidence protocol: clean-room provenance, explicit assumption-to-call-site reasoning, composed production-function properties, non-vacuity controls, deliberately false claims, mutation sensitivity, counterexample-led repair, and cryptographically bound reproducibility evidence.

1563:6. checking final verdict totals;
1592:> The completed SUB-T6 case study independently evaluated the production `mlk_poly_sub → mlk_poly_reduce → mlk_poly_tomsg` slice of `mlkem-native` under the ML-KEM-768 caller bounds. Five frozen harnesses established object/separation conditions, subtraction representability, exact coefficient-wise subtraction, frame preservation, reduction handoff, canonical `tomsg` input, const-input behavior, and bounded safety. The positive models produced 2,343 successful CBMC property results with no failures or unknowns. Twelve symbolic cover goals confirmed boundary and index reachability, three deliberately false claims were rejected with isolated counterexamples, and four preregistered mutants were killed through five detector executions. The result does not constitute a complete proof of `mlk_indcpa_dec` or ML-KEM. Its contribution is the caller-oriented property decomposition and the evidence protocol used to evaluate AI-generated formal artefacts against a frozen, already high-assurance implementation.
